# IACS_Monitor — Code Review Remediation Tracker

Review date: 2026-09-30 · Baseline: 470 tests passing · Out of scope by owner decision: API authentication.

Tier 1 completed 2026-09-30 — 28/28 Fixed. Tier 2 completed 2026-09-30 — 14/14 Fixed. Tier 3 completed 2026-10-01 — 44/44 Fixed. Missing features (F-01–F-20) and follow-ups (N-xx) completed 2026-10-01. Suite 470 → 1414 tests passing, coverage 90%.

Status key: **Open** · **In progress** · **Fixed** (with test or verification noted) · **Won't fix** (with reason)

Tiers: **T1** = data loss, broken install, or wrong security results · **T2** = UI · **T3** = robustness/scale · **F** = missing feature / remaining work

---

## T1 — Install, deploy, schema

| ID | Issue | Location | Status | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1-01 | `install.sh` deletes the production database on the `cp` fallback path (no rsync) | install.sh:132 | **Fixed** | no rm of user data; rsync and tar fallback share one exclude list (`*.db*`, `.env`, …). Unversioned DBs are stamped at baseline and reconciled, not deleted. |
| T1-02 | Alembic chain cannot build a DB from empty; root migration assumes 15 tables already exist | alembic/versions/946998cf677b | **Fixed** | new root `0001baseline0` builds the full schema; all later revisions idempotent; new `d4e5f6a7b8c9` adds any columns/indexes a legacy create_all DB lacks. Test: `test_upgrade_head_on_empty_db`, zero drift vs models. |
| T1-03 | `init_db()` `create_all` never stamps Alembic version; later `alembic upgrade head` fails "table already exists" | app/db.py:83-104 | **Fixed** | `init_db` stamps head on a new DB; warns with `alembic stamp 0001baseline0 && alembic upgrade head` for unversioned DBs; warns when behind head. install.sh / docker-entrypoint.sh stamp + upgrade automatically. |
| T1-04 | PostgreSQL migration alters `forti_analyzer_events`; real table is `fortiana_events` | alembic/versions/b4c5d6e7f8a9:79,111 | **Fixed** | targets `fortiana_events`. |
| T1-05 | `postgresql_where='is_current IS 1'` invalid for boolean on PostgreSQL | alembic/versions/946998cf677b:82,122 | **Fixed** | `is_current = true` (PG) / `= 1` (SQLite). PostgreSQL SQL rendered offline and reviewed (no PG server available to execute). |
| T1-06 | `requirements.txt` missing `reportlab` (PDF report) and `psycopg2` (PostgreSQL) | requirements.txt | **Fixed** | `reportlab==4.4.10`, `psycopg2-binary==2.9.13`; new `requirements-dev.txt` (pytest, pytest-cov, httpx). Use `postgresql+psycopg2://` URLs. |
| T1-07 | Docker image omits `alembic/`, `alembic.ini`, `generate_report.py`; container DB can't be migrated | Dockerfile:59-61 | **Fixed** | image copies alembic/, alembic.ini, generate_report.py; `docker-entrypoint.sh` runs `alembic upgrade head` before uvicorn; `.dockerignore` added. |

## T1 — Findings engine

| ID | Issue | Location | Status | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1-10 | Closed/acknowledged findings can never re-fire: `source_ref` is globally unique but dedup only checks open findings; IntegrityError swallowed. Test at test_alert_engine.py:206 masks it | models.py:1324, alert_engine.py:161-240 | **Fixed** | partial unique index `ux_findings_source_ref_open` (open only). Migration closes pre-existing duplicate open findings (oldest kept, note added; 2 found in the real DB). Masking test rewritten. |
| T1-11 | Existing open finding never updated when severity escalates (e.g. traffic confirms a rule gap) | alert_engine.py Phase 6 + `_make_finding` | **Fixed** | `_escalate_if_higher()` in `_make_finding` updates severity/title/detail/updated_at on the existing open finding; escalations are logged, not counted as new. |
| T1-12 | `_rule_zone_purdue` keys on interface name, rules store zone names; multi-interface zones (`port1,port2`) and `firewall_id=None` rules never resolve; FirewallZone fallback hard-codes None | alert_engine.py:3198-3223 | **Fixed** | `_RuleZoneResolver` (built once per run) resolves zone names via FirewallZone membership, comma/space lists, alias/zone_label, `any` = all levels on that firewall, `firewall_id=None` via device name. |
| T1-13 | `_parse_rule_port`: `any`→None (allow-any rules skipped), ranges take lower bound only, `80 443` / `eq 22` / service names → None, ICMP port 0 flagged | alert_engine.py:3263 | **Fixed** | `_parse_rule_ports()` returns ranges; handles any, ranges, lists, Cisco eq/range/gt/lt/neq, service names, legacy list reprs, `dst:src`. Checks test overlap; one finding per rule listing matched ports; ICMP/port 0 skipped. |
| T1-14 | `_rule_traffic_confirmed` called without Purdue/zone filters and ignores TCP vs UDP — unrelated traffic escalates rules to critical | alert_engine.py:3306,3756 | **Fixed** | confirmation requires session endpoints at the rule's resolved src/dst levels, matching transport and a matched port; one query per rule; wording now “matching traffic observed”. |
| T1-15 | `review_rule_conduit_drift` ignores approved entries with `dst_port=None` (any port) | alert_engine.py:3733-3754 | **Fixed** | any-port approved entries cover all ports (scoped by conduit/transport); baseline_id in dedup key (also closes T3-21). |
| T1-16 | Six Phase 6 checks and helpers have no tests | tests/ | **Fixed** | `tests/test_rule_twins.py` (every check, port-parser table, resolver, escalation, recurrence, no-duplicate reruns). |

## T1 — Firewall parsing

| ID | Issue | Location | Status | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1-20 | Disabled rules stored as active permits (FortiOS `set status disable`, pfSense `<disabled>`, PAN-OS `disabled yes`) | forti_config_parser.py:525, firewall_parser.py:171,247 | **Fixed** | Stored with `enabled=false` (FortiOS status disable, pfSense `<disabled/>`, PAN-OS `disabled yes`, ASA `inactive`) and excluded from every analysis (reachability, shadowing, rule twins, cross-FW, topology diff/unused, FortiOS config checks). Flagged for removal by new check `disabled_rule` (checklist 3.8, SR 7.7): one finding per logical rule, low; medium if a permit that would open IT→OT or is any-any. Stat `rules_disabled`; `GET /firewall-rules?enabled=false`; `disabled_rule_count` on devices. Migration `e5f6a7b8c9d0`. Tests: `tests/test_disabled_rules.py` (exclusions mutation-checked). |
| T1-21 | Negated address rules stored un-negated (FortiOS `srcaddr-negate`, pfSense `<not/>`, PAN-OS `negate-source`) | same | **Fixed** | permit + negation → negated side becomes `any` with `[negated …]` note in raw_line; deny + negation skipped (`rules_skipped_negated_deny`). |
| T1-22 | Panorama pre/post-rulebase not matched; fallback `.//rules/entry` ingests NAT/decryption rules as permits | firewall_parser.py:238-240 | **Fixed** | only security rulebases (shared pre, DG pre, local, DG post, shared post); generic fallback removed; missing action skipped (`rules_skipped_no_action`). |
| T1-23 | FortiOS `rule_order` = policy ID instead of config order (`move` reorders) | forti_config_parser.py:2393 | **Fixed** | rule_order = position in config (verified VDOM flattening preserves order). |
| T1-24 | FortiOS multi-range service stored as `"['502', '102']"`; `dst:src` range stored raw | forti_config_parser.py:425-429 | **Fixed** | canonical ports, one row per protocol; service groups and predefined services resolve; NTP/SNMP/SMB definitions corrected. |
| T1-25 | Cisco object-groups mis-tokenised (src=`object-group`, dst=group name) | firewall_parser.py:55-64 | **Fixed** | IOS/ASA network, service, port and protocol object-groups plus `object network/service`, nested, expanded src×dst×svc; unresolved → `object-group:NAME`. |
| T1-26 | Cisco sequence-numbered entries and ASA `access-list X extended` lines dropped; standard ACLs garbled; ipv6 ACL lines attributed to previous ACL | firewall_parser.py:47 | **Fixed** | sequence numbers, remarks, ASA `extended`/`line N`, standard ACLs, trailing keywords; wildcard vs netmask detected by bit pattern; IPv6 sections skipped (`rules_skipped_ipv6`). |
| T1-27 | Cisco dst_port stored as `"eq 22"`/`"eq telnet"`; `eq www`, `neq` unparsed | firewall_parser.py:72 | **Fixed** | canonical `dst_port` (`any`, `22`, `80,443`, `1000-2000`); port-name table; unknown names → `unknown:NAME` (never widened to any); pfSense `a:b` and aliases; PAN-OS service objects, `application-default`. |

## T1 — Reachability, shadowing, attestation

| ID | Issue | Location | Status | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1-30 | Implicit deny ignored: `no_match` never treated as deny | reachability.py:559-576 | **Fixed** | no match on a firewall with rules = denied (`reason_code: implicit_deny`); zero rules loaded = `no_policy`. |
| T1-31 | Port-specific rules match when no port queried; a lone `permit tcp/443` reads as fully permitted | reachability.py:195-202 | **Fixed** | no-port queries return `permitted` / new value `partial` (with `permitted_services`, `denied_services`) / `denied`; port queries match only when protocol and port ranges contain the query. |
| T1-32 | Rule direction/zones ignored (IT→OT rule also permits OT→IT) | reachability.py | **Fixed** | rule direction from zones/interfaces, then address subnets, else bidirectional. Newer Firewall model supported, including single firewalls with 3+ legs (every level pair; one crossing per physical firewall). |
| T1-33 | One IPv6 rule crashes `find_shadowed_rules` for every device (TypeError in `subnet_of`); IPv6 rule stops IPv4 evaluation in reachability | rule_shadow.py:95, reachability.py:168 | **Fixed** | IPv4/IPv6 compared only within version; IPv6 rules skipped for IPv4 queries; `0.0.0.0/0`/`::/0` = any; per-device errors isolated (`devices_failed`). |
| T1-34 | Second attestation for same checklist+baseline always 409 (insert before demote) | routes.py:2366-2393 | **Fixed** | demote then insert; IntegrityError → 409 only for a genuine race. Test: `tests/test_attestation_api.py`, verified end-to-end. |
| T1-35 | Attestation with no baseline demotes attestations for every baseline; NULL-baseline rows bypass the uniqueness index | routes.py:2386-2391 | **Fixed** | NULL-baseline demote scoped to `baseline_id IS NULL`; new partial unique index `ix_attestation_one_current_nobaseline` (migration demotes pre-existing duplicates). |

| T2-06 | Inventory save sends `hostname`/`zone` (dropped), `critical` rejected (400), reads `vlan_id` not `vlan` | index.html inventory | **Fixed** — sends only accepted fields/values; reads `vlan`; save errors shown. |
| T2-07 | Firewall Devices reads zones/policies/interfaces the API doesn't return; every card shows 0 | index.html:1087-1098 | **Fixed** — real active/disabled counts, interfaces, boundary editing, rules drill-down via new `?firewall_device_id=`. |
| T2-08 | `fw_topology.html` workflow (pcap-fw, confirm-link, set-upstream) missing from index.html; old pages still required | index.html | **Fixed** — full FW Topology Builder section in index.html (pcap-fw, confirm, sides, upstream, suggest-purdue, run-checks). |

| T2-11 | Stored XSS via `proof_filename`, `attested_by`, `notes` | attestation.html:292,322 | **Fixed** — attestation.html filename/attested_by/notes rendered as text. |
| T2-12 | `API_BASE` hard-coded to `http://localhost:8000` | upload.html:108 | **Fixed** — all pages default to the serving origin (localhost only when opened from disk). |
| T2-13 | Upload forms lack `firewall_device_name`, `purdue_zone_from/to`, syslog `device_name`; FortiGate form sends old `device_name` param | upload.html | **Fixed** — every upload form sends current handler params; stats table incl. new skip/disabled keys; error detail shown; forti-event JSON added. |
| T2-14 | Findings pages fetch without `limit` (default 200) so counts are wrong; `closed` styled as open | findings.html:273, index.html | **Fixed** — counts from `/findings/summary` (+ new `by_status`); real paging via new `offset` + `X-Total-Count`; open/acknowledged/closed styled distinctly. |

## T3 — Robustness, scale, correctness

| ID | Issue | Location | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| T3-01 | Sync parsing inside `async def` handlers blocks the event loop (health check 42 s during PCAP upload) | routes.py:84,2620, syslog/report/baseline routes | **Fixed** — parse/DB work in `run_in_threadpool` for every ingest route; test proves /health < 1 s during a 15k-packet PCAP ingest. |
| T3-02 | `_read_limited` buffers the whole upload before checking size; attestation proof bypasses it | routes.py:33-41,2348 | **Fixed** — `_spool_upload` streams 1 MB chunks, 413 at first chunk over limit (incl. attestation proof); request-body cap middleware (`IACS_MAX_REQUEST_BYTES`). |
| T3-03 | Unbounded list endpoints; `/findings?limit=-1` returns all rows | routes.py:608,741,983,1016,1052,1455,2290 | **Fixed** — limit/offset + `X-Total-Count` on all list endpoints; `/topology` bounded, edges only between returned nodes, truncation keys/headers; export streamed. |
| T3-04 | `/findings/export` firewall_id filter done in Python after loading all rows; misses string IDs | routes.py:1446-1460 | **Fixed** — firewall_id filter in SQL (`->>` / `json_extract`), int and string stored values match. |
| T3-05 | `/findings/export?until=DATE` excludes that whole day | routes.py:1437 | **Fixed** — date-only `until` includes the whole day (export and summary export). |
| T3-06 | Re-uploading the same syslog file duplicates every FortiAnalyzerEvent | routes.py:2066 | **Fixed** — `dedup_hash` (unique index) on events; `ingest_syslog_line_ex` / `ingest_forti_event_json_ex`; routes report `duplicates_skipped` and skip evaluation of duplicates. |
| T3-07 | Naive DateTime columns with aware `utcnow()`; PostgreSQL shifts by server TZ | models.py:72 | **Fixed** — all timestamp columns use `UTCDateTime`: stored as naive UTC on every backend (no PostgreSQL server-TZ shift), returned timezone-aware UTC. Rows written to PostgreSQL before this fix may already be shifted (N-18). |
| T3-08 | SQLite `foreign_keys` off, no WAL | db.py | **Fixed** — `configure_sqlite_engine()`: pysqlite savepoint fix, `foreign_keys=ON`, WAL for file DBs, busy_timeout. |
| T3-09 | Missing indexes: `protocol_details.session_id`, `network_sessions.dst_host_id`, `last_seen` | models.py | **Fixed** — indexes on protocol_details.session_id, network_sessions.dst_host_id, hosts/sessions last_seen. |
| T3-10 | 5-tuple session index not unique; concurrent ingests duplicate sessions | models.py | **Fixed** — unique `ux_session_5tuple`; migration merges existing duplicates; `_get_or_create_session` is race-safe. |
| T3-11 | `/topology/firewalls` N+1 lazy load | routes.py:3005 | **Fixed** — `selectinload` for firewall interfaces. |
| T3-12 | Internal exception text returned to client | routes.py:142,190,279,311,315,329,2512,2749,2920 | **Fixed** — unexpected errors logged with an error id; clients get a generic message + id; deliberate validation messages kept. |
| T3-13 | `ingest_pcap` commits partway; failed `/ingest/pcap-fw` leaves partial data | pcap_parser.py:408 | **Fixed** — `ingest_pcap(commit=False)` / `check_unauthorized_industrial_writes(commit=False)`; routes commit once or roll back (internal commits deferred). |
| T3-14 | Scheduler starts once per uvicorn worker | main.py:74-99 | **Fixed** — `fcntl.flock` single-instance guard (`IACS_SCHEDULER_LOCK`); only the holder runs the scheduler. |
| T3-15 | Scan window uses `received_at` (insert time) not `event_time`; historical uploads trigger false scans; `ingested_at` fallback references missing column | alert_engine.py:759-778 | **Fixed** — scan window keyed on `event_time` (fallback received_at); bogus `ingested_at` reference removed. |
| T3-16 | `evaluate_event` broad except: on PostgreSQL one SQL error aborts the transaction for all later checks | alert_engine.py:879 | **Fixed** — each real-time check in its own savepoint (`_run_isolated`). |
| T3-17 | `run_periodic_reviews` has no per-review isolation; one failure aborts the run; docstring mentions nonexistent `_baseline` param | alert_engine.py:3802 | **Fixed** — per-review savepoints; failures reported via `failed_reviews` (also in `/reviews/run` response); docstring corrected. |
| T3-18 | Phase 6 N+1 queries (EXISTS per rule, zone map rebuilt 4×) | alert_engine.py Phase 6 | **Fixed** — per-run traffic index + twin run context: 2063 → 13 SELECTs for a 500-rule ruleset. |
| T3-19 | Finding explosion: one policy → src×dst×svc rows → duplicate findings; re-ingest recreates rule IDs → duplicate findings; `Finding.rule_id` FK has no ondelete | alert_engine.py, forti_config_parser.py:2375 | **Fixed** — one finding per logical rule per check, dedup on stable `rule_key` (survives re-ingest; superseded per-row findings closed); `Finding.rule_id` ON DELETE SET NULL. |
| T3-20 | One IT→L1 Telnet rule yields four overlapping findings | alert_engine.py Phase 6 | **Fixed (by design)** — distinct checklist rows kept; each finding carries `related_checks`; distinct titles; decision documented in module docstring. |
| T3-21 | `rule_conduit_drift` dedup key lacks baseline_id | alert_engine.py | **Fixed** (with T1-15) |
| T3-22 | CEF with BSD syslog prefix (`<134>Sep 30 12:00:00 fw1 CEF:`) not recognised; falls to kv parser as empty event | forti_parser.py:78-80 | **Fixed** — CEF found behind RFC3164/RFC5424 headers; no empty events. |
| T3-23 | FortiOS CEF keys (`FTNTFGTpolicyid`, `dvchost`, `FTNTFGTeventtime`) unmapped; protocol numeric | forti_parser.py:100-138 | **Fixed** — FTNTFGT* keys, `dvchost`, numeric protocols, `cat` mapped. |
| T3-24 | CEF policy_id precedence bug: `cs1` used as policy_id when cs1Label is something else | forti_parser.py:107-108 | **Fixed** — csN only via labels; FTNTFGTpolicyid first. |
| T3-25 | CEF values with spaces truncated; escaped quote in kv value lets log text overwrite srcip/action | forti_parser.py:48-51 | **Fixed** — proper CEF extension parsing; escaped quotes honoured; first key wins (no field injection); JSON built directly. |
| T3-26 | Timestamps: `eventtime` ignored, epoch s/ns wrong, `tz=` ignored, oversized `rt` raises OverflowError aborting ingest | forti_parser.py:62-73 | **Fixed** — eventtime (any precision) → date+time+tz → rt/start/end; UTC; never raises. |
| T3-27 | CSV: preamble with "Device"/"Action" taken as header; BOM; "Destination Port" unmapped; `type=` accepts any line | forti_parser.py:143-233 | **Fixed** — best-header detection, BOM, column variants, surplus cells kept; non-FortiGate kv lines rejected. |
| T3-28 | PCAP stream stalls forever after one misaligned segment (S7/DNP3); no seq tracking | pcap_parser.py:131-143,352-356 | **Fixed** — per-direction TCP reassembly (seq order, retransmits, gaps), protocol-aware resync, 1 MiB cap; coalesced Modbus decoded. |
| T3-29 | IP-only Host never merged when pcap later sees same IP with MAC; routed IPs get gateway MAC/vendor | common.py:156 | **Fixed** — IP-only/MAC-only hosts merged; gateway MACs detected and not stamped on remote IPs. |
| T3-30 | Session/host first/last_seen use ingest time not packet time; IPv6 skipped; analyzer exceptions abort ingest | pcap_parser.py:326-369 | **Fixed** — packet timestamps; IPv6 counted; analyzer errors isolated and counted. |
| T3-31 | DNP3 g1v1 packed bits read as bytes; ENIP REAL/unsigned decoded as signed int | dnp3_analyzer.py:243, enip_cip_analyzer.py:246,271 | **Fixed** — DNP3 packed bits (g1v1/g10v1/g80v1), qualifier 0x28; CIP values decoded by type code. |
| T3-32 | Baseline port ranges/lists become None (treated as any) | baseline_parser.py:152 | **Fixed** — ranges stored as dst_port + `dst_port_end`, lists split into rows, bad rows rejected (never widened to any); consumers honour dst_port_end. |
| T3-33 | Traffic checks: one critical per packet incl. responses; S7 plc_stop/download and DNP3 restart not flagged; MAC-only ApprovedHost ignored | traffic_findings.py:51,136-183 | **Fixed** — requests only, one finding per operation with occurrence count; S7 stop/program and DNP3 restart/stop flagged; MAC-only ApprovedHost matched. |
| T3-34 | Multi-hop session falls back to every rule in topology_diff; rule order and denies ignored | topology_diff.py:325-395 | **Fixed** — sessions evaluated along the reachability path (every hop, first-match, denies count); `policy_mismatch` / `reason_code` / `blocking_rule`. |
| T3-35 | `range:a-b` converted to an under-covering CIDR | topology_diff.py:126 | **Fixed** — exact address ranges (`AddrRange`) in rule_match, used by shadow, reachability and diff. |
| T3-36 | Client with no prior class gets `protocol_inferred` + level None, outranking VLAN inference; source port can make client the "server" | device_classification.py:722,741 | **Fixed** — server side by canonical/well-known port only; client-only hint ranks lowest. |
| T3-37 | Firewall VLAN propagation excludes NULL source and never reads `SwitchVlan.purdue_level` | device_classification.py:614-670 | **Fixed** — NULL sources included (manual untouched); `SwitchVlan.purdue_level` used first. |
| T3-38 | rule_shadow: `0.0.0.0/0` not treated as any; zone strings compared literally; exception rules flagged as partial shadows; single severity for all shadow types | rule_shadow.py:90-93,179,296-304,452 | **Fixed** — 0.0.0.0/0 = any; zones as sets; exception rules reported info-only; per-type shadow severity passed to findings. |
| T3-39 | cross_fw: any-any check ignores port/zone; asymmetry compares raw strings; deny naming interface missed with zone_label; PAN-OS/IOS not implicit-deny | cross_fw_findings.py:177,218,274,360 | **Fixed** — any-any needs src+dst+service any on link zones; asymmetry normalised and link-scoped; deny matched by interface/zone label/alias; implicit-deny vendors info only. |
| T3-40 | Upstream firewall chains can form cycles (A→B→A) | topology_manager.py:271-276 | **Fixed** — cycles rejected with the chain named; chain walks use a visited set. |
| T3-41 | Shadow O(n²) with 64k-element sets for `gt 1023`; reachability re-parses per level pair; classify_hosts loads all rows; topology_diff unordered `limit(10_000)` | various | **Fixed** — rule parse cache; column-only batched classify (1.55 s → 0.59 s); deterministic diff ordering + truncation reported (71 s → 3.8 s on 50k sessions). |
| T3-42 | `make-dist.sh` .sha256 stores absolute path; tarball omits generate_report.py; stale dist/ checked in | make-dist.sh | **Fixed** — checksum records the bare file name (verified with `sha256sum -c` elsewhere); tarball includes generate_report.py, docs, sample_data, requirements-dev; stale dist/ removed. |
| T3-43 | `install.sh` ignores `IACS_HOST`/`IACS_PORT` from .env (systemd ExecStart hard-coded) | install.sh | **Fixed** — systemd unit uses EnvironmentFile + `${IACS_HOST}`/`${IACS_PORT}` |
| T3-44 | Base image not pinned by digest; junk in source root (db, pdf, pytest cache, dist) | Dockerfile, repo root | **Fixed** — base image pinned to `python:3.11.10-slim-bookworm` (digest pin via `PYTHON_BASE` build-arg, documented); `.dockerignore` + `.gitignore`; build/test junk removed (operator DB and report left in place, ignored). |

## F — Missing features / remaining work

| ID | Item | Status |
|---|---|---|
| F-01 | Test coverage: 36/47 alert_engine public functions untested; ~50 routes without HTTP tests; generate_report, compute_diff, ingest_firewall_config untested | **Fixed** — 470 → 1414 tests; coverage 85% → 90% (no module < 80%); every route has success + error HTTP tests with a guard test that fails on unlisted routes; all 55 public alert_engine functions have positive + negative tests; end-to-end sample ingest snapshot. Six app bugs found and fixed (pfSense/PAN-OS hint 500, phantom firewall device, NTP finding dedup collision, phantom host from MAC-table re-upload, substring zone matching ×2). |
| F-02 | `generate_report.py` uses hard-coded SAMPLE_FINDINGS; lists 39 of 53 checks | **Fixed** — `generate_report.py` reads the database (read-only), CLI `--db/--out/--baseline/--include-closed/--site`; check table derived from CHECK_TYPE_REFERENCE; attestation, firewall inventory, coverage (no data vs no findings); `GET /reports/compliance.pdf`. |
| F-03 | README out of date (module tree, UI pages, env vars, CORS advice, report generator) | **Fixed** — README rewritten from the code (routes, 14 env vars, inputs, generated checks table, UI, deploy, migrations, report, development, module tree, limitations). |
| F-04 | Auto-close findings when the causing rule/traffic disappears; update evidence/last_seen on open findings | **Fixed** — re-detection updates `last_seen` / `occurrence_count`; successful reviews auto-resolve findings no longer detected (22 reviews; partial-input runs skip; `IACS_AUTO_RESOLVE`); config re-ingest auto-resolves config findings; acknowledged findings absorb recurrences and reopen on escalation (closed ones re-fire). |
| F-05 | NAT (VIP/SNAT) modelling in reachability and diff | **Fixed** — `nat_rules` table; NAT parsed for FortiOS (VIP/vip6/groups, policy SNAT + ippool), PAN-OS, pfSense, Cisco IOS, ASA; applied hop-by-hop in host path queries and topology diff with per-vendor pre/post-NAT policy matching; `GET /nat-rules`; UI shows translations. Not covered: FortiOS central SNAT, VIP46/64; level matrices ignore NAT. |
| F-06 | Address/service object resolution: pfSense aliases, PAN-OS objects, `application-default` | **Fixed** — pfSense aliases, PAN-OS address + service objects/groups (shared/device-group/vsys scopes), `application-default`, Cisco object-groups resolved. |
| F-07 | IPv6 support end-to-end (pcap, rules, reachability) | **Fixed** — IPv6 end to end: PCAP (extension headers, fragments, NDP, multicast handling), rules stored for every vendor, v4/v6-aware matching (families never cross), reachability/shadow/diff, host path queries. |
| F-08 | Purdue L3.5 DMZ level (levels are Integer; validation 0–4 vs 0–5 inconsistent) | **Fixed** — Purdue L3.5 Industrial DMZ first-class: `app/purdue.py` single source of truth; level columns NUMERIC(2,1) with IN-set checks (migration b9c0d1e2f3a4); API accepts 3.5/"L3.5"/"dmz"; adjacency/violation logic, classification, alert semantics (DMZ→OT management one severity lower), UI everywhere. |
| F-09 | Host identity across ingests (DHCP/IP change), deletion tracking | **Fixed** — `host_address_history` + `hosts.stale`; DHCP renumbering moves a MAC's host to its new IP (merging IP-only duplicates, FKs re-pointed); IP→new MAC recorded as conflict; `mark_stale_hosts`; `GET /hosts/{id}/history`, `POST /hosts/mark-stale`; pcap/ARP/MAC-table wired. |
| F-10 | Rule-log support: write `FirewallRule.last_matched`; extract sentbyte/rcvdbyte/duration | **Fixed** — `FirewallRule.last_matched` written from syslog rule hits (FortiOS policy id, PAN-OS rule name, pfSense tracker, ASA first-match) and from sessions in topology diff; unused rules exclude rules matched within `IACS_UNUSED_RULE_DAYS`; bytes/duration in raw_fields for every vendor. |
| F-11 | Non-Fortinet syslog parsers (Cisco ASA, pfSense filterlog, PAN-OS) | **Fixed** — Cisco ASA/FTD, pfSense/OPNsense filterlog, PAN-OS TRAFFIC/THREAT/SYSTEM/CONFIG parsers; `POST /ingest/syslog` with per-line vendor auto-detect, dedup, real-time checks and rule hits. |
| F-12 | Modbus TCP reassembly, IP defrag, DNP3 transport reassembly, DNP3 qualifier 0x28 | **Fixed** — Modbus coalesced requests, IPv4/IPv6 defragmentation, DNP3 transport-layer reassembly, DNP3 qualifier 0x28. DNP3 over UDP 20000 still not dispatched. |
| F-13 | Per-packet DB queries in `get_or_create_host` (large pcap performance) | **Fixed** — host cache, bulk ProtocolDetail inserts, direct frame decoding: 30k packets 65 s → ~1.8 s. |
| F-14 | Delete endpoints (CORS allows DELETE, none exist) | **Fixed (gated)** — DELETE for findings, attestations, firewall devices, baselines, hosts; disabled unless `IACS_ENABLE_DELETE=true` because the API has no authentication yet; every delete logged. |
| F-15 | PAN-OS `set vsys`/Panorama `set device-group`, quoted rule names; FortiOS `policy6`/internet-service; Cisco `ip access-group` binding | **Fixed** — PAN-OS vsys / Panorama device-group set paths and quoted names; FortiOS policy6 and internet-service tokens; Cisco `ip access-group` / ASA `access-group` bindings recorded as zones. |
| F-16 | `run_purdue_suggestions` never clears stale suggestions; locally administered MAC detection in OUI lookup; Cisco dotted MAC in LLDP | **Fixed** — Purdue suggestions recomputed each run and require real evidence; locally administered / multicast MAC detection (`mac_kind`); dotted/hyphen/bare MACs normalised incl. LLDP/CDP; scapy OUI fallback fixed (was always None). |
| F-17 | Expose `_unknown_check_name_count`; dev requirements (pytest, coverage) | **Fixed** — `GET /diagnostics` (unknown check names, last review run); `requirements-dev.txt` (pytest, pytest-cov, httpx, pypdf, playwright). |
| F-18 | Rule twins should use rule src/dst CIDRs for traffic confirmation | **Fixed** — rule-twin traffic confirmation requires session IPs inside the rule's concrete src/dst addresses. |
| F-19 | Multi-site phase 3: syslog from devices of several sites, unknown devices need a map-to-site function | **Fixed** — device registry `site_devices` + held-line queue `unmapped_log_events` (migration f1a2b3c4d5e6, `app/site_devices.py`); config uploads auto-register hostnames (cross-site conflicts reported, never moved); log routes route every line by serial / device name, unknown devices held without checks, `?site=` maps unknown devices only (conflicts counted); `/site-devices` CRUD with replay, unmapped list / discard / purge, daily retention (`IACS_UNMAPPED_RETENTION_DAYS`); console Devices page. Tests: `tests/test_sites_phase3.py`. |
| F-20 | Multi-site phase 4: retention, roll-up dashboard, per-site PDF, docs | **Fixed** — `GET /sites/rollup` (read-only, grouped queries; per-site counts, open findings by severity, last review / upload, active baseline, attestation state, global unmapped count, totals); console "Sites overview" page with click-through and per-site PDF link; per-site PDF cover shows site name + code (`site_code`) and is isolation-tested, `all_sites=true` = summary + one section per site; unmapped-line retention verified and documented (no automatic deletion of findings/events); README Sites section final pass. Tests: `tests/test_sites_phase4.py`. |

## Added during Tier 1 work

| ID | Item | Status |
|---|---|---|
| N-01 | UI must handle new reachability value `partial` and keys `permitted_services`, `denied_services`, `reason_code`, `multi_leg` (fold into T2-03) | **Fixed** (with T2-03) |
| N-02 | Finding escalations are logged but not returned by the review/ingest API responses | **Fixed** — `escalations` / `auto_resolved` in `/reviews/run`, escalations + `rule_hits_updated` in log-ingest stats. |
| N-03 | PAN-OS address objects/groups not resolved (service objects now are) | **Fixed** (with F-06). |
| N-04 | IPv6 members inside pfSense aliases / Cisco object-groups dropped without a counter | **Fixed** — IPv6 members now stored (F-07); mixed-family pairs counted (`mixed_family_pairs_skipped`). |
| N-05 | Reachability: when two firewalls each permit only some hosts, host scopes are shown per device but not intersected; multi-address fields treated as unresolved; legacy FirewallDevice rows with no linked Firewall still bidirectional | **Fixed** — host scopes intersected across hops; multi-address fields parsed as sets; legacy devices infer direction from zone/host subnets before falling back. |
| N-06 | Migration `d4e5f6a7b8c9` targets live `Base.metadata` (additive only). All future migrations must stay idempotent (use `alembic/iasc_migration_utils.py`) | Convention |
| N-08 | UI: show disabled rules (`enabled` field, `?enabled=false`, `disabled_rule_count`) on firewall/rule pages (fold into T2-07) | **Fixed** (with T2-07) |
| N-07 | SQLite legacy DBs: FKs on columns added by the reconcile step are not created (SQLite limitation; not enforced there anyway). PostgreSQL path verified only by offline SQL render | Known limitation |

| N-09 | MAC-table auto-detect failed on real multi-line dumps (row regex anchored without MULTILINE) → 422 on the bundled Cisco sample | **Fixed** — `re.MULTILINE`; regression test |
| N-10 | `/topology` edges carry no server-side violation flag and nodes no hostname; UI derives edge class from host levels | **Fixed** — `/topology` edges carry `violation` + `crosses_firewall`; nodes carry `hostname`. |
| N-11 | `POST /topology/set-interface-side` cannot clear a side/level (null = unchanged) | **Fixed** — `set-interface-side` accepts a `clear` list. |
| N-12 | `/findings` has no text-search parameter; UI search filters the current page only | **Fixed** — `GET /findings?q=` server-side search; both findings UIs use it. |
| N-13 | `/ingest/firewall` does not range-check `purdue_zone_from/to` server-side (UI does) | **Fixed** — server-side Purdue range checks on `/ingest/firewall` and `/ingest/fortigate-config` via `app/purdue.py`. |
| N-14 | UI browser smoke suites: `tests/ui_demo_server.py` (seeds a demo DB with XSS payloads and serves it), `tests/ui_smoke_index.py`, `tests/ui_smoke_pages.py` — run each against a freshly seeded DB; not collected by pytest | Convention |

| N-15 | `review_conduit_matrix_drift` reports sessions with no matching firewall rule (via topology_diff) but never compares traffic against the baseline's ApprovedCommsEntry; a true session-vs-baseline drift check is missing | **Fixed** — `conduit_matrix_drift` compares traffic with the active baseline's approved comms; old rule-coverage check kept as `session_no_permitting_rule`. |
| N-16 | UI does not yet show `/diff` `sessions_truncated` or `/topology` `truncated` / `hosts_total` | **Fixed** — truncation notices in the console and `topology.html`. |
| N-17 | Each FortiAnalyzer event now runs ~11 savepoints (check isolation); adds statement overhead on bulk syslog ingest | **Fixed** — one savepoint per event, per-check savepoints only on failure (e.g. 18 → 14 statements per IT→OT event). |
| N-18 | PostgreSQL rows written before T3-07 may be offset by the server's TimeZone; not correctable automatically | Known limitation |
| N-19 | Docker image build not exercised here (no Docker in the build environment); verify `docker build` once | Partly verified — image builds and runs (migrations, non-root user, /health, /ui, PDF route, healthcheck) on a locally built stand-in base; pulling the real `python:3.11.10-slim-bookworm` base is blocked by this environment's network policy, so run `docker build` once where Docker Hub is reachable. |
| N-20 | `tests/conftest.py` points tests at a temp DB and fails the run if `./iasc_monitor.db` is modified (a test had opened it and switched it to WAL; restored byte-identical) | Convention |

| N-21 | Acknowledged findings now count as active for dedup: a persisting condition updates the acknowledged finding (last_seen / occurrence_count) instead of opening a duplicate; escalation reopens it with a note; only closed findings re-fire as new | **Changed (design)** |
| N-22 | `unused_policy` keyed on the logical rule (stable across config re-ingest), one finding per policy, unused only if no expansion matched | **Fixed** |
| N-23 | Category mislabel: `unauthorized_industrial_write` documented as `traffic_review` but stored as `periodic_review` (category filter returned nothing) | **Fixed** |
| N-24 | HP switch config uploaded with `vendor_hint=cisco` is parsed as Cisco instead of rejected; `_lookup_vlan_name` in mac_table_parser is dead code; same policy id in two VDOMs keeps only the later one | **Fixed** (tests/test_n24_n26.py, tests/test_n24_vdom.py): vendor_hint contradicting content is rejected with 422 (`check_vendor_hint`, switch and MAC table); `_lookup_vlan_name` wired into MAC ingest (same-site VLANs; names returned in stats, not stored); policy identity is now (firewall, vdom, policy id) with `vdom` column (migration a2b3c4d5e6f7), re-ingest replaces per VDOM |
| N-25 | NAT not modelled: FortiOS central SNAT, VIP46/VIP64, IPv6 internet-service; level-pair matrices ignore NAT (no concrete addresses) | **Fixed** (tests/test_n25_nat.py): central-snat-map, VIP46/VIP64 (+policy46/64), IPv6 internet-service parsed; level-pair cells/paths are NAT-aware (`translated_scopes`, `nat_dependent`, `nat_notes`); cross-family-only cells report unknown, not denied. Assumptions: central NAT on disables per-policy NAT rows; `nat disable` exemptions over-approximate; VDOMs evaluated separately, inter-VDOM links not followed |
| N-26 | DNP3 over UDP/20000 not dispatched to the analyzer; IPv4 multicast/broadcast destinations still create host rows | **Fixed** (tests/test_n24_n26.py): DNP3 over UDP/20000 dispatched (per-datagram framing, malformed counted in `dnp3_udp_malformed`); IPv4 multicast/limited broadcast and IPv6 multicast destinations create no Host/session; non-unicast sources create no host. Directed broadcast not handled (subnet unknown) |

## Audit 2026-10-01 (independent re-audit)

| ID | Sev | Issue | Location | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A-01 | High | CSRF: cross-origin form POSTs accepted (attestation, ingest, reviews, mark-stale); a malicious page can forge attestations via an operator's browser | app/main.py, routes.py Form/File routes | Open |
| A-02 | High | Exponential group expansion (diamond address/service groups) — 1 KB config can freeze the server; no memoisation or size cap | firewall_parser.py:487,1321,1585; forti_config_parser.py:379 | **Fixed** — resolved groups memoised (diamond 30 levels ≈ 0.01 s); caps 10k members/group, 50k rows/rule, depth 64; over-limit sides become `too-large:<name>` (treated as unresolved, never any); stats `groups_over_limit`, `rules_over_limit` |
| A-03 | High | docker-compose publishes the unauthenticated API on all interfaces ("8000:8000") | docker-compose.yml:9 | **Fixed** — compose binds `${IACS_BIND_ADDR:-127.0.0.1}:8000`; README notes the opt-in |
| A-04 | High | Permit with unresolved service object is skipped for port queries and falls to implicit deny (reports denied instead of unknown); unresolved deny skipped so later permit reads permitted | reachability.py:1065-1078 | **Fixed** — every rule whose match can't be decided makes the result `unknown` (host paths and matrices); `too-large:` tokens included |
| A-05 | High | Pre-NAT vendors (PAN-OS, Cisco IOS): port-forward permit on the external VIP dropped by direction inference | reachability.py:876,913-938 | **Fixed** — on pre-NAT vendors a rule dst inside a DNAT ext_ip takes the mapped address's level for direction |
| A-06 | High | Level-pair matrix ignores SNAT; downstream hops permitting only the translated source show denied | reachability.py:1239,111-123 | **Fixed** — matrices translate permitted scopes through each hop's SNAT/DNAT; inexact translation gives `partial`, never `denied` |
| A-07 | High | FortiOS `set action ipsec` stored as deny (policy VPNs permit traffic) | forti_config_parser.py:781 | **Fixed** — `ipsec` → permit (noted); unknown actions skipped (`rules_skipped_unknown_action`) |
| A-08 | High | pfSense `<type>match</type>` (non-terminal) stored as enabled deny-any | firewall_parser.py:1402 | **Fixed** — pass→permit, block/reject→deny, match/unknown skipped (`rules_skipped_match`); floating quick/non-quick noted |
| A-09 | High | Rule-twin reviews never mark partial zone resolution → live findings auto-resolved when a zone stops resolving | alert_engine.py rule-twin reviews / _AUTO_RESOLVE_CHECKS | **Fixed** — auto-resolve is per finding: rule-keyed findings whose rule wasn't fully evaluated this run are kept (rule twins, unused_policy) |
| A-10 | High | Non-tcp/udp baseline entry (e.g. ICMP) covers every port of a tcp/udp rule in conduit drift; transport-any rule covered by tcp-only entry | alert_engine.py:5117-5134 | **Fixed** — baseline entries cover a port only if protocol is any or matches the transport; transport-any rules need tcp and udp coverage; `uncovered_by_transport` |
| A-11 | High | IT/OT bypass checks ignore L4/L5 → L2 (only fire for L0–L1 targets) | alert_engine.py:528, ~5011, review_ns_* | **Fixed** — L4/L5 ↔ L2 now flagged as bypass (events/sessions high; rule twins medium, high with traffic); L0–L1 cases and dedup keys unchanged; DMZ→L2 stays with mgmt/remote-access checks |
| A-12 | Medium | shadow_rule dedup key lacks rule ids/device → distinct shadow pairs merge (Cisco/pfSense/PAN-OS; same policy ids across firewalls) | alert_engine.py:1993-2020 | **Fixed** — key = logical rule identity of both rules + device/firewall; old open findings migrated to the new key |
| A-13 | Medium | review_shadow_rules doesn't mark incomplete when devices_failed → auto-resolves that device's findings | alert_engine.py:1925 | **Fixed** — devices_failed marks the run incomplete (no auto-resolve) |
| A-14 | Medium | IPS / remote-access-app dedup ignores attack_name / app; auth_from_non_admin_host suppresses forever | alert_engine.py:756,804,851 | **Fixed** — IPS keyed per attack (severity follows event), remote-access per app, non-admin auth per source/device/UTC day |
| A-15 | Medium | Keyword substring false positives: 'temp' in 'attempt', 'add' in 'addr', 'fan', 'power' | alert_engine.py:824,1082 | **Fixed** — whole-word / structured-field matching (logid, cfgpath, action, ha subtype) |
| A-16 | Medium | traffic_findings dedup ignores acknowledged; no savepoint on insert; several check names (unauthorized_industrial_write, config_*, cross_fw_*) not in CHECK_TYPE_REFERENCE | traffic_findings.py:230; models.py | **Fixed** — acknowledged counts as active, savepoint inserts, re-detection counters; 56 missing check names registered + scan test |
| A-17 | Medium | Review row limits apply to sessions not flows, unordered → findings randomly missed | alert_engine.py:1495,1598,1657 | **Fixed** — reviews aggregate distinct flows in SQL with deterministic order before limiting |
| A-18 | Medium | Malformed Cisco address (10.050.1.0) / Cisco NAT → uncaught ValueError, whole upload 500 | firewall_parser.py:442,454,662,2261 | **Fixed** — bad ACE/member/NAT line dropped and counted (`lines_rejected`, `nat_lines_rejected`, `reject_reasons`) |
| A-19 | Medium | Cisco object-group chain ~3000 deep → RecursionError (no depth limit) | firewall_parser.py:487-499 | **Fixed** — depth limit covers every Cisco path; tests for 3000-deep chains |
| A-20 | Medium | Malformed FortiOS blocks crash ingest (str/list type errors; list bound to DB) | forti_config_parser.py:257,320,541,1060,1710,2143,2569,2868,3069 | **Fixed** — FortiOS cleanup pass; bad blocks counted (`blocks_rejected`); fuzz test |
| A-21 | Medium | FortiOS config findings can collide on source_ref within one ingest (autoflush off) → IntegrityError aborts upload | forti_config_parser.py:2934-2948,3069 | **Fixed** — in-ingest source_ref tracking; duplicates update instead of aborting |
| A-22 | Medium | Gateway-MAC detection quadratic; one spoofed off-subnet IP marks any MAC a gateway | common.py:263-265 | **Fixed** — set-based (200k IPs < 2 s); gateway needs ≥2 off-subnet IPs, >8 IPs, or IPv6 RA |
| A-23 | Medium | Asset-inventory CSV: invalid MAC + no IP crashes (host None); multicast/broadcast accepted as hosts | baseline_parser.py:345-347 | **Fixed** — invalid/multicast/broadcast identities rejected (`rows_rejected`) |
| A-24 | Medium | topology_diff fallback: any firewall's permit 'explains' a session with no path; last_matched inflated by alternative paths/scopes | topology_diff.py:215-252,512-517 | **Fixed** — fallback limited to firewalls that plausibly carry the flow (else `no_enforcement_point_known`); last_matched stamped only on deciding rules |
| A-25 | Medium | No firewall on a segment breaks path finding (flat L2/L3 → no_policy) | reachability.py:1364-1403 | **Fixed** — inferred flat adjacency between unfirewalled levels; guesses yield unknown, never denied (`inferred_adjacency`) |
| A-26 | Low | Proof download trusts client MIME, no X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff | routes.py:2973,3054-3064 | **Fixed** — MIME allowlist, attachment, nosniff everywhere; CSP/X-Frame-Options/Referrer-Policy on UI |
| A-27 | Low | install.sh chowns code/venv to the service account | install.sh:237 | **Fixed** — code/venv root-owned; data dir only writable; DB moved on upgrade |
| A-28 | Low | site/search filters don't escape LIKE wildcards % and _ | routes.py:951,1111 | **Fixed** — shared `_like_pattern()` escaping |
| A-29 | Low | ASA syslog timezone abbreviations (EST) ignored; device name lost | syslog_vendors.py parse_syslog_header | **Fixed** — ~45 timezone abbreviations/offsets parsed; hostname kept |
| A-30 | Low | No cap on number of TCP streams in reassembly | pcap_parser.py:1109,1267 | **Fixed** — 50k streams / 256 MiB caps, LRU eviction (`streams_evicted`) |
| A-31 | Low | Shadow analysis pairwise only (union coverage missed), same-order rows compared, O(n²) per device | rule_shadow.py:417-459 | **Fixed** — union coverage → single full_shadow; same-order rows skipped; 5k rules ~330 s → 8.8 s |
| A-32 | Low | UI lists silently capped at 10,000 rows (no X-Total-Count check); concurrent review runs can override lifecycle changes | ui/index.html:1542,1715,2451; alert_engine.py | **Fixed** — UI pages through X-Total-Count; concurrent review runs skipped (process lock; PG advisory lock) |

| ID | Sev | Issue | Location | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| B-01 | High | Legacy upgrade aborts (SQLite left half-migrated at b9c0d1e2f3a4) when a legacy DB has duplicate firewall names or >1 active baseline: d4e5 creates ux_firewalls_site_name/ux_baselines_site_active before c0d1 backfills site_id (verified) | alembic c0d1e2f3a4b5:133, d4e5f6a7b8c9, e1f2 _resolve_sql | **Won't fix** (owner: code always runs as a fresh install; legacy-DB upgrade paths are out of scope. Fresh `alembic upgrade head` still must match the models) |
| B-02 | High | Cubic ReDoS in ASA 106100 regex: ~10KB line hangs a worker ~20 min (verified: 2000 spaces = 10s) | syslog_vendors.py:_ASA_106100 | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_quick_a.py): linear ASA 106100 tail; 4 more super-linear syslog regexes fixed (106001, 106015, 113004/5, 733100); MAX_LINE_CHARS=8192 |
| B-03 | High | Quadratic `^\s*` MULTILINE regexes on whole-file text (config hostname extraction on every upload, switch/mac parsers): 20k blank lines = 6s (verified) | site_devices.py:241, switch_parser.py:559-623, mac_table_parser.py:48,56 | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_quick_b.py): `^[ \t]*` in switch/mac/arp/panos detectors; linear hostname lookup; timing tests on 20k blank lines and 200KB inputs |
| B-04 | High | One unrelated FortiAnalyzer event in 24h silences all pcap-based review_ns_* bypass checks (gate has no type/device filter) (verified in code) | alert_engine.py:3182 | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_batch2_a.py): FAZ gate removed; a pcap finding is suppressed per flow only when an open real-time FAZ finding AND a permitted FAZ traffic event for the same site/flow exist |
| B-05 | High | Real-time checks treat only accept/allow as permitted, so session-end logs (close/timeout/client-rst, incl. normalised ASA teardown) never raise IT/OT bypass etc. | alert_engine.py:595,672,784,1046 | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_a.py): `_PERMITTED_ACTIONS`/`_is_permitted` used at all accept/allow sites; accept+close dedups to one finding |
| B-06 | High | Topology diff evaluates only first 10,000 sessions (ORDER BY id LIMIT) silently; other reviews cap 5-10k rows silently | topology_diff.py:431; alert_engine.py:1647-1970 | **Fixed** (test_audit_batch2_a.py): topology diff and all nine capped reviews page through every row; result reports sessions_evaluated/truncated; any residual cap blocks auto-resolve and shows in last_review_info()['truncated'] |
| B-07 | High | FortiOS `config system zone` never resolved: zone-named policies apply both directions, giving wrong denied/permitted | forti_config_parser.py:732, reachability.py:995 | **Fixed** (tests/test_fortios_group1.py, _followup.py): `config system zone` and SD-WAN zones stored in firewall_zone_defs (per firewall+VDOM); one shared resolver (app/parsers/fortios_zones.py) used by reachability, shadow, topology diff, alert_engine, cross-FW and topology_manager; intrazone default deny; unknown zone -> unknown, empty zone matches nothing |
| B-08 | High | DoS: no aggregate FirewallRule row budget (28KB config -> 200k rows, 1.3GB); gzip pcap accepted with size limit on compressed bytes; no host/session cap | firewall_parser.py:123, pcap_parser.py:973, routes.py:420 | **Fixed** (tests/test_b08_limits.py, test_followup_hostrace_crossfw.py): per-upload rule-row budget IACS_MAX_RULE_ROWS_PER_INGEST=100k (413, nothing stored; per-rule cap 25k; batched writes); compressed/non-pcap uploads 415; pcap caps IACS_PCAP_MAX_PACKETS=2M / _NEW_HOSTS=20k / _SESSIONS=200k / _SECONDS=600 (partial commit, `truncated` in response); race-safe cached host insert (ON CONFLICT); per-site pcap ingest lock on PostgreSQL. 5k new hosts 15.8s -> 5.7s |
| B-09 | High | SQLite long upload killed by any concurrent small commit (deferred BEGIN; 'database is locked' reported as 400 'nothing stored') | db.py:~96 | **Fixed** (test_audit_batch2_b.py, _c.py): BEGIN IMMEDIATE for write requests and background jobs (WriteSessionLocal/get_write_db); IACS_SQLITE_BUSY_TIMEOUT_MS (30s); remaining lock errors -> 503 + Retry-After |
| B-10 | High | PostgreSQL review advisory lock leaks after commit; later reviews skipped permanently | alert_engine.py:5992-6012 | **Fixed** (test_audit_batch2_a.py; also run against a throwaway PG 16): advisory lock held on a dedicated connection for the whole run, released on that connection in finally; connection discarded if unlock fails |
| B-11 | Med | Device routing: unknown serial falls back to name and lands in another site's device; held-line dedup key ignores routed device/serial (data loss on map); name-only vendors (pfSense/ASA) cross-route silently | site_devices.py:99-113,356,432 | **Fixed** (tests/test_b11_conflicts.py, ui smoke devices): owner decision = hold and let the user choose. First serial seen is bound to a serial-less device; serial conflicts and ?site mismatches are held as conflicts (unmapped_log_events.kind; migration c4d5e6f7a8b9) and resolved on the Devices page (same device / different device at a chosen site / discard), with replay and a recorded resolution (device_conflict_resolutions); held-line dedup key includes device key and serial. Source-IP pinning skipped: uploaded files carry no network source IP |
| B-12 | Med | delete_attestation 'promote previous' has no site filter (promotes other site's row; can 500 on unique index) | routes.py:4286 | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_b.py): promotion limited to same site/checklist/baseline, skipped if a current row exists |
| B-13 | Med | Central-SNAT `nat disable` exemption not modelled (false 'denied'); _load_nat has no order_by | forti_config_parser.py:1311, reachability.py:1852,777 | **Fixed** (test_fortios_group1.py): NAT rows carry `sequence` and `nat_exempt`; first-match in config order everywhere (incl. /nat-rules); a matching `nat disable` entry means no translation |
| B-14 | Med | Broadcast/multicast gaps: DNP3/UDP to broadcast/multicast not decoded (bypasses write check); directed broadcast and group MACs create phantom hosts; MAC-table ingest not de-duplicated | pcap_parser.py:~1435, host_identity.py:62, mac_table_parser.py:226 | **Fixed** (test_audit_batch2_b.py, _c.py): group-destination DNP3 decoded via a per-site group marker host (excluded from host lists, counts and unknown-device review); directed broadcast/group dst MACs create no hosts; MAC table skips group/CPU rows and upserts on re-ingest |
| B-15 | Med | FortiOS parse gaps: re-ingest leaves removed interfaces/zones; NAT lists truncated mid-token; invalid port ranges become any/any (false permit); tokenizer mishandles escaped quotes and multi-line values | forti_config_parser.py:2951,1381-1388,704,112-144 | **Fixed** (test_fortios_group1.py): re-ingest deletes interfaces/zones no longer present (per VDOM; operator annotations on a deleted interface go with it); NAT columns Text, no truncation; invalid port ranges -> `unresolved:` token; tokenizer handles escaped quotes, multi-line quoted values, keywords only at statement start |
| B-16 | Med | Finding status endpoint: reopen with open recurrence 500s; stale acknowledged_by/closed_at persist and block auto-resolve; no validation/length caps; cross_fw _upsert_finding same stale fields | routes.py:2417, cross_fw_findings.py:156 | **Fixed** (test_audit_batch2_b.py, _c.py): reopen with an open recurrence -> 409 with open_finding_id; reopen (manual, escalation, cross-FW, config auto-resolve) clears ack fields and closed_at; status/acknowledger/notes validated (422) |
| B-17 | Med | Attestation accepts blank attested_by and future dates (hides finding) | routes.py:3424 | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_b.py): blank attested_by and future attested_at (UTC) rejected with 422 |
| B-18 | Med | CSV exports not formula-escaped (=HYPERLINK, =cmd|) from network-sourced fields | routes.py:~1380,2247 | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_b.py): `_csv_safe` on all CSV writers |
| B-19 | Med | check_log_silence matches substring 'logd' (in every FortiGate logdesc): false findings | alert_engine.py:1226 | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_a.py): field-value word-boundary match; `logdesc` no longer triggers |
| B-20 | Med | Non-RFC1918 addressing (IPv6, ULA, CGNAT, link-local) skipped/mislabelled by real-time checks; hosts without Purdue level skipped without _mark_incomplete | alert_engine.py:151,1744,1799 | **Fixed** (test_audit_batch2_a.py): _is_internal_ip/_is_public_ip cover IPv6, IPv4-mapped, ULA, CGNAT, link-local (documentation ranges treated as public); hosts with no Purdue level counted in skipped_unknown_level and only their findings are held from auto-resolve |
| B-21 | Med | Destructive routes not gated by IACS_ENABLE_DELETE: unmapped purge/discard, mark-stale without site | routes.py:~4850 | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_b.py): purge/discard behind IACS_ENABLE_DELETE; mark-stale is single-site (422 if ambiguous) |
| B-22 | Med | Migration hygiene: b2c3 downgrade drops baseline columns; no pre-upgrade backup in entrypoint and stale _alembic_tmp_* breaks retry; legacy DBs lack FKs/CHECK/Text types; PG lacks pool_pre_ping; PG-only drift (GIN indexes, FK name); passing a connection with FK=ON breaks batch rebuild | alembic/, db.py, docker entrypoint | **Won't fix** (owner: code always runs as a fresh install; legacy-DB upgrade paths are out of scope. Fresh `alembic upgrade head` still must match the models) |
| B-23 | Med | UI: site-selector races (no sequence guard in index.html loaders, apiAll mixes sites); keyboard-inaccessible rows/headers/topology nodes | ui/index.html:1177-1334,1892,2109,2478,3197 | **Fixed** (test_group4_api_ui.py, ui_smoke b23): loaders capture site once and drop stale responses (AbortController + sequence); keyboard-operable rows, nodes, cards; sort headers are buttons with aria-sort; focus restored after re-render |
| B-24 | Low | /assets `site` param is free-text location (ilike across sites); ?site=² and ids > 2^63 return 500; hostile upload filenames (NUL, >255) 500; check_vendor_hint case-sensitive; findings/summary by_site always cross-site; many reviews missing from _AUTO_RESOLVE_CHECKS; junk Host rows from address objects; /32 mask parsed as 0.0.0.0/0 in legacy rows | routes.py:1133,1299; sites.py:83; misc | **Fixed** (test_audit_quick_b.py, test_fortios_group1.py, test_group4_api_ui.py): `?site=²`, oversized ids -> 422, hostile filenames, case-insensitive vendor hint, junk hosts from address objects; /assets uses `location` for free text and `site`/`site_id` as the real site filter; /findings/summary by_site honours site_id; 8 reviews added to auto-resolve (5 review_ns_*, 3 VLAN) and cross-FW findings auto-close (dedup key now includes both firewalls, VDOM and policy id). /32-mask legacy-row parsing: Won't fix (fresh install only) |
| B-25 | Med | HA clusters: FGCP members share a hostname but log with their own serial, so the second unit was held as a serial conflict and the cluster looked like two devices (follow-up to B-11) | app/site_devices.py, models | **Fixed** (tests/test_ha_clusters.py, ui smoke devices): one device with many member serials (site_device_serials, migration d5e6f7a8b9c0, max IACS_HA_MAX_MEMBERS=4); HA detected from `config system ha`; a new member is added on evidence (HA logs 0108037892/37894 field `sn` from a known member, or pre-registered serial), otherwise held as `ha_candidate` with an 'Add as HA member' action; later evidence replays held lines; never across sites; `ha_failover` (low) on a-p clusters only |

## Audit 2026-10-04 (post B-series re-audit; C-01/02/03/05 re-verified by hand, others reproduced by reviewers unless noted)

| ID | Sev | Issue | Location | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C-01 | High | Session orientation breaks flow identity: NetworkSession src/dst = first packet seen; conduit drift, review_ns_it_ot_bypass and siblings key on (src,dst,dst_port), so a mid-flow capture of one approved IT->OT:502 flow yields findings for :50001/:50002/... (FP storm) that FAZ per-flow suppression never matches (verified) | pcap_parser.py:544, alert_engine.py:1639,3506,3595,3685 | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_c_engine.py): shared canonical_flow(client,server,server_port) used in conduit drift, review_ns_* keys and _FazCoverage; three mid-stream captures of one flow collapse to one finding and match the FAZ finding |
| C-02 | High | Port-forward VIP with extport != mappedport -> false 'denied': rule row keeps the external service port while evaluation runs on post-NAT dst ip:port (verified) | forti_config_parser.py:1264, reachability.py:2064 | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_c_fortios.py): VIP port-forward policies translate the service port through extport->mappedport; equal/unequal/range tested |
| C-03 | High | One unescaped quote inverts quote pairing for the rest of the config: policies after it vanish silently (0 blocks_rejected, no warning) (verified) | forti_config_parser.py:137 _read_quoted | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_fortios.py): _read_quoted requires a newline-crossing closer to be followed by whitespace/EOL else EOL-terminates; stray quote no longer drops later policies; counted in stats.quote_recoveries |
| C-04 | High | Central-SNAT orig-port/nat-port ignored: a `nat disable` exemption limited to tcp/80 exempts every TCP flow -> wrong first-match, false 'denied' downstream | forti_config_parser.py:1476, reachability.py:609 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_fortios.py): central-snat orig-port parsed onto NatRule.orig_port and tested in snat_matches; a tcp/80 exemption no longer exempts tcp/443 (migration e6f7a8b9c0d1) |
| C-05 | High | Zone names containing a space are split into tokens that match nothing -> deny applies both ways, false 'denied' and false shadow findings (verified) | fortios_zones.py:37, rule_shadow.py:180, reachability.py:1067 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_fortios.py): zone srcintf/dstintf split on comma only, multi-word zone names match whole; no false deny, no false shadow |
| C-06 | High | Ambiguous name-only held lines are replayed into whichever same-name device is next touched (no-op POST or PATCH /site-devices) — violates 'never guess' | site_devices.py:1066 _pending_for | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_c_registry.py): held lines route only on an unambiguous match (serial, or name unique across sites); a same-name-at-multiple-sites serial-less line stays held; no-op create/update no longer replays |
| C-07 | High | HA evidence spoofable: 37892 hostname ignored, missing ha_group skips the group check, and a line can bind its own devid on first sight and immediately serve as evidence -> `devid=EVIL sn=VICTIM` adds another site's unit as a member | site_devices.py:652-673,734,881-920 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_registry.py): HA evidence requires hostname==device name when present and matching ha_group when known, and a line that just first-sight-bound a serial can't vouch for a peer |
| C-08 | Med | session_no_permitting_rule fires per flow when a site has no firewall rules at all (should be _mark_incomplete) | topology_diff.py:753, alert_engine.py:1714 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_engine.py): session_no_permitting_rule marks the review incomplete and emits nothing when the site has no firewall policy at all |
| C-09 | Med | Interface alias equal to a zone name wins over the zone def -> false 'denied' | reachability.py:1076 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_fortios.py): _zone_levels resolves a token against zone defs first, then exact interface names, then alias/label |
| C-10 | Med | Reachability quadratic in rule rows (list membership on ipaddress tuples): 21k rows under the caps -> compute_reachability 216s | reachability.py:1544 _evaluate_device_core, _combine_verdicts | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_fortios.py): reachability dedup/membership now via hashable sets; 4000 rules ~0.8s (was hundreds of seconds) |
| C-11 | Med | ha_failover dedup hashes only window_hour (two clusters or A->B->A in one hour collapse); out-of-order 37892 state logs raise false failovers; member cap bypass via POST /site-devices creating a second same-name row | alert_engine.py:1428,299; site_devices.py:814,379 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_engine.py, test_audit_c_registry.py): _dedup_key hashes site_device_id+to_serial; member-activity ordering is last-write-wins by log timestamp; a second same-name HA row is refused (409) |
| C-12 | Med | SQLite pool exhaustion: pool_timeout (30s) == busy_timeout; queued writers exhaust the pool and GETs return plain-text 500 (sqlalchemy TimeoutError unmapped) | db.py:146 | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_c_dbapi.py): pool-checkout TimeoutError -> 503 + Retry-After; pool_size/max_overflow/pool_timeout configurable with pool_timeout < busy_timeout |
| C-13 | Med | Five text-upload routes 500 on csv.Error (random bytes / UTF-16); /ingest/fortigate-config accepts any text (200, registers a device, 11 compliance findings from 'hello world') | baseline_parser.py:92, routes.py:3337-3466,736 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_dbapi.py): csv.Error/UnicodeDecodeError and UTF-16/32 BOM -> 422 (nothing stored); /ingest/fortigate-config rejects non-FortiGate text (422) instead of registering a device + 11 bogus findings |
| C-14 | Med | auth_failure dedup ignores `user` (docstring says per user); _redetected overwrites evidence, erasing the first user | alert_engine.py:299,996,457 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_engine.py): auth_failure dedup includes user; _redetected merges evidence instead of overwriting |
| C-15 | Med | FAZ per-flow suppression window uses received_at not event_time: a bulk upload of 10-day-old events suppresses current pcap evidence | alert_engine.py:3413 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_engine.py): FAZ per-flow suppression windows on coalesce(event_time, received_at); stale bulk events no longer suppress current pcap findings |
| C-16 | Med | PostgreSQL string overflows: identity strings (devid/serial) never truncated vs String(128); FirewallRule.src_zone/dst_zone String(128) overflow on 14-interface policies; assets fields unbounded -> DataError, whole upload fails and is mislabelled 'numeric out of range' | site_devices.py:140, models.py src_zone/dst_zone, routes.py:362,1425 | **Fixed** (test_audit_c_fortios.py, test_audit_c_registry.py, test_audit_c_dbapi.py): FirewallRule.src_zone/dst_zone widened to Text (migration e6f7a8b9c0d1); over-long devid/serial/devname held as malformed; assets string params length-capped; DataError mapping distinguishes string-too-long from numeric overflow |
| C-17 | Low | GET handlers still write on the deferred session (/diff last_matched, /firewall-rules/shadowed?generate_findings, /sites) -> SQLITE_BUSY_SNAPSHOT under concurrent writers | topology_diff.py:622, rule_shadow.py:901, routes.py:4918 | **Fixed** - the three GETs do their writes on a write-capable session (`_write_session`, resolved through the app's dependency overrides so test harnesses are honoured): /diff runs on it, /firewall-rules/shadowed only when `generate_findings`, /sites only to create the Default site |
| C-18 | Low | site_scope gaps: Core select/update/delete(X.__table__) and db.get() of a warm identity-map object are unfiltered; main.py reuses one session across site runs (warm-identity leak risk); a Finding built in scope A with a site-B session gets site_id A | site_scope.py, main.py:92, sites._fill_site_ids | **Fixed (guarded)** - scheduler `expunge_all()`s between site runs; a flush-time row whose parent is in another site than the bound one logs a warning naming both (the row follows its parent); the remaining bypasses (Core statements, `Session.get` of warm objects) are documented in docs/multi-site-design.md with the in-scope helpers to use |
| C-19 | Low | Registry edges: resolve orphans identity-less ('', serial) rows; POST with a known serial renames the device; different_device allowed on name-only site_mismatch via API; purge deletes pending conflict lines with no audit row; concurrent resolves can double-replay (no conditional status update); UI ignores replay result after Remap | site_devices.py:1106,354,1218,1439,1165; ui/index.html:1735 | **Fixed (part)** - replay claims each held row with a conditional UPDATE (pending → replayed) so concurrent resolves cannot double-replay, and a resolve whose rows were just claimed returns 409 (tests/test_b11_conflicts.py::test_replay_claims_rows_once); Remap now shows the replay result. Left as designed: a known serial with a new hostname renames the device (a config upload after a hostname change is the common case; `check_unambiguous` guards it), purge already keeps pending conflict lines, identity-less rows take the serial as name |
| C-20 | Low | review_ns_mgmt_from_non_admin not paginated (N+1); PATCH status overwrites notes (loses auto-resolve/escalation trail); /ingest/forti-event 500 on non-UTF-8 non-JSON body; UTF-16 uploads decode as garbage | alert_engine.py:3897, routes.py:2709,3134 | **Fixed** - `review_ns_mgmt_from_non_admin` one joined keyset query; PATCH status appends a stamped note (`[time who -> status] text`, newest kept whole at 2048) instead of overwriting; a non-UTF-8 request body is a 422 (`UnicodeDecodeError` handler) not a 500; UTF-16/32 config, syslog and switch uploads are transcoded by their BOM (CSV keeps its explicit 422) |

## Scenario review 2026-10-04 (multi-firewall: 12 FortiGates in 6 serial pairs, 6 HP switches, Teleport vendor path)

Found by driving the live API on a scratch database.

| ID | Sev | Issue | Location | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D-01 | High | Access-path enumeration never finishes on a 10-firewall chain: `itertools.product` over every firewall's candidate policies (15^10 combinations, 1.6M intersections in 40 s before the profile was stopped) | access_paths.py `_enumerate` | **Fixed** (tests/test_access_paths.py `test_walk_stages_prunes_dead_branches`): depth-first walk with the running intersection prunes dead branches; same row order; 354 rows in 0.5 s |
| D-02 | High | A data interface with `allowaccess https ssh` and no FortiOS `role` was classed management-only even when policies use it; it then dropped out of link proposals and Purdue suggestions (6 of 12 downlinks) | forti_config_parser.py `_classify_interface` | **Fixed** (tests/test_audit_c_fortios.py): an interface referenced by a policy (directly or via zone) is never management-only on allowaccess alone; a mgmt name/alias/zone still is |
| D-03 | High | `POST /topology/run-checks` 500 (UNIQUE findings.site_id, source_ref): one policy expands to several rows with the same source_ref and sessions run with autoflush off, so the dedup lookup missed the pending row | cross_fw_findings.py `_upsert_finding` | **Fixed** (tests/test_cross_fw_findings.py): flush after insert |
| D-04 | High | `it_ot_bypass` flag is suppressed whenever the path crosses L3.5, so a routed L4 -> L1 chain through the DMZ firewalls (no session break) is not flagged, while its L3 -> L1 tail is. Contradicts the confirmed definition (L3/L3.5/L4/L5 -> L0/L1, L4/L5 -> L2) | access_paths.py `_compute_flags`; tests/test_access_paths.py::test_multi_firewall_chain_single_row asserts the exemption | **Fixed** - direct routed rows crossing the DMZ firewalls are bypass; only composed rows are exempt (test flipped) |
| D-05 | High | Rule-level reviews (`rule_it_ot_bypass`, mgmt-wrong-zone family) compare interface zone levels per firewall, so a chain of firewalls that each touch adjacent levels is never seen (0 findings for IT -> PLC-02 through 8 firewalls); only the session-based check and the Access Review catch it | alert_engine.py `review_rule_it_ot_bypass` | **Fixed** - `app/parsers/chain_findings.py`: `chain_it_ot_bypass` / `chain_mgmt_wrong_zone` raised from access-path rows whose chain spans ≥ 2 firewalls (one finding per bypassing sub-chain, dedup on tag key, whole chain in evidence, auto-resolve, skipped when direction unverified); tests/test_chain_findings.py |
| D-06 | Med | `cross_fw_asymmetric_permit` compares rules across every confirmed link, including a shared segment between two different boundaries' firewalls: 62 of 68 findings were noise (same rule on both firewalls of a pair, or traffic that never crosses both) | cross_fw_findings.py `_check_asymmetric_permits` | **Fixed** - compared only for serial pairs (same interface-level set; unknown levels keep the old behaviour) and never for permits whose destination sits on the link segment; only the planted asymmetries remain; `asymmetric_skipped_links` in the run summary |
| D-07 | Med | Link proposals: 60 'low' proposals (every pair of 172.16.x.0/30 transit nets share a /16); an interface holds one peer, so a shared DMZ segment with three firewalls cannot be recorded | topology_manager.py `_overlap_confidence`, models | **Fixed** - shared /16 no longer proposes a link (`low` tier removed); ≥ 3 firewalls on one network become a shared-segment proposal, confirmed with `POST /topology/confirm-segment` into `FirewallInterface.segment` (migration b0c1d2e3f4a5); cross-firewall checks pair every member; both topology pages show segment proposals and badges |
| D-08 | Med | Remote-access path Vendor -> Teleport proxy -> DMZ jumpbox -> OT is not shown end to end: the Teleport -> jumpbox hop stays inside one segment, so no firewall row joins them (only the direct vendor RDP rule composes to OT) | access_paths.py `_compose_conduits` | **Fixed** - operator-declared hop (`access_path_hop`, migration a9b0c1d2e3f4, `/access-paths/hops`, UI panel, suggestions); composed rows carry `declared_hop` |
| D-09 | Med | `session_remote_access_bypass` trusts only baseline `jump_host` entries by destination: once the Teleport proxy and jumpbox are approved, vendor RDP straight to the jumpbox (bypassing Teleport) stops alerting; `device_type=jump_host` on the host is ignored by the check | alert_engine.py `review_ns_remote_access_bypass` | **Fixed** - `remote_access_gateway` role + device_type jump hosts; new path 2b (external → jump host not via gateway, high) |
| D-10 | Low | Protocol inference puts an RTU/VFD/gateway at L1 and a SCADA server that also receives DNP3 as `rtu_or_ied`; HMIs on a control VLAN land at L1; Purdue suggestions then follow the wrong levels until hosts are labelled by hand | device_classification.py | **Fixed** - Modbus server → `modbus_device` (L1, type open) instead of `plc`; a host polling ≥ 2 distinct controllers is supervisory (`local_scada`, L2) even if it also serves DNP3 (an RTU answering one master stays `rtu_or_ied`); a polling host on a "control" VLAN is not pushed below L2 |
| D-11 | Low | Interface purdue levels unset: access-path rows silently come out with reversed direction (HMI -> PLC shown as L1 -> L2 'up'), no warning | reachability/access_paths | **Fixed** - rows built from a policy with no level evidence on either side carry `direction_unverified`; the envelope's `unlabelled_firewalls` names the firewall and interfaces; Access Review notice + flag legend, report legend; chain findings skip such rows |

## Audit 2026-10-05 (code audit of the declared-hop / gateway-role / bypass work; three independent reviewers, each finding re-verified by hand before fixing)

Scope: `remote_access.py`, `access_paths.py` composition and `_walk_stages`, the `/access-paths/hops` routes, both remote-access checks in `alert_engine.py`, the migration, the Access Review hops panel and the report. The general sweep (raw SQL, uploads, XML, ORDER BY, regex, site scoping of id routes, error leakage, autoflush, UI sinks, subprocess, deserialisation) found nothing new beyond the open A-01 / C-18.

| ID | Sev | Finding | Where | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| E-01 | Med | Hop and label lookups compared addresses as stored (`Host.ip` / `ApprovedHost.ip` are kept as ingested) with the canonical form of the request, so a host ingested as `2001:DB8:0:0:0:0:0:1` was "unknown" for `2001:db8::1`, and gateway / jump-host labels in non-canonical IPv6 never matched events or sessions | remote_access.py `_host`, `gateway_ips`, `jump_host_ips`; alert_engine.py both remote-access checks | **Fixed** (`canon_ip`; labels and comparisons canonical; tests/test_declared_hops.py::test_hop_accepts_non_canonical_addresses, test_cov_alert_engine.py::test_check_remote_access_bypass_knows_gateways_and_host_labels) |
| E-02 | Med | Real-time (FAZ) `check_remote_access_bypass` still used baseline `jump_host` rows only: a VPN tunnel or external HTTPS terminating on the declared gateway was a finding, a `device_type=jump_host` host was not a jump host, and path 2b did not exist there - inconsistent with the session fallback fixed under D-09 | alert_engine.py `check_remote_access_bypass` | **Fixed** - same labels (`remote_access.jump_host_ips` / `gateway_ips`), gateway sanctioned in paths 1 and 2, path 2b added (high) |
| E-03 | Low | `AccessPathHop.__repr__` copied from `AccessPathTag` referenced attributes the model lacks → `AttributeError` inside SQLAlchemy error messages and test diffs | models.py | **Fixed** |
| E-04 | Med (PG) | `service` validated to 20 tokens can reach 319 chars; `String(255)` truncation error on PostgreSQL surfaced as 500 after validation passed | remote_access.py `normalize_service` | **Fixed** - 255-char cap → 422 (test_service_edge_cases) |
| E-05 | Low | Suggestions offered gateway → jump host pairs that `create_hop` then refused (baseline address with no host row) | remote_access.py `suggest_hops` | **Fixed** - unknown hosts skipped (test_suggestions_skip_baseline_only_addresses_and_tolerate_role_spelling) |
| E-06 | Low | Two concurrent declarations of the same pair both passed the pre-check; the loser hit the unique index → 500 instead of 409 | remote_access.py `create_hop` | **Fixed** - IntegrityError → HopError 409 (test_duplicate_race_is_409) |
| E-07 | Low | Hop matching (`_single_host(addr_intersect(...))`) ran per (leg1, leg2) pair although each side depends on one leg only: O(pairs × hops) interval work | access_paths.py `_compose_conduits` | **Fixed** - per-leg memo of matching hop indexes, intersected per pair |
| E-08 | Low | Hops reference hosts by IP with no staleness signal: a deleted / re-levelled host left a hop composing rows on a broken premise, silently | remote_access.py `list_hops`; UI panel | **Fixed** - `from_known` / `to_known` / `level_mismatch` on `GET /access-paths/hops`; "Re-check" status column in the panel (test_list_hops_flags_stale_hops) |
| E-09 | Low | `PATCH /hosts/{id}/role` is free-form; `Remote-Access-Gateway` or `REMOTE_ACCESS_GATEWAY` silently never counted as a gateway | remote_access.py `_role_is` | **Fixed** - case and `-`/`_` insensitive match for both roles |
| E-10 | Low | `"any,tcp/22"` was rejected instead of collapsing to any | remote_access.py `normalize_service` | **Fixed** |
| E-11 | Med | Access Review: a failed `GET /access-paths/hops` rendered as "No hops declared." while the paths table still showed hop-joined rows; re-declaring then returned 409 | ui/index.html `loadAccessReview`, `renderArHops` | **Fixed** - error kept and shown in the panel (`ar-hops-error`), summary says "could not load" |
| E-12 | Med | No busy guard on Declare / Remove: a double submit stored the hop and then printed "Not saved: HTTP 409"; a double Remove printed "Not removed: HTTP 404" for a hop that was gone | ui/index.html | **Fixed** - one write at a time (`arHopBusy`), buttons disabled meanwhile |
| E-13 | Low | Declare with no site selected (or after a failed load) did nothing, silently; a write could also target the previously loaded site after a site switch mid-click | ui/index.html | **Fixed** - explicit messages; write refused when the selected site differs from the loaded one |
| E-14 | Low | Stale "Hop declared; paths re-computed." survived a site switch and was printed even when the reload failed | ui/index.html | **Fixed** - message cleared on site change / empty state; "re-computed" only when the reload rendered the same site |
| E-15 | Low | Hop glyph differed between UI (`→`) and report / finding label (`⇒`); `→` already means "firewall-permitted leg" | ui/index.html | **Fixed** - UI uses `⇒` for the declared step |
| E-16 | Low | Report printed raw flag names with no legend; `declared_hop` / `conduit_through_dmz` unexplained. Mapping `declared_hop` to an SR was considered and rejected: it records how a composed row was built (the sanctioned 4.1 pattern), a bad row is condemned by its other flags | generate_report.py | **Fixed** - flag legend footnote; comment on `_AP_FLAG_CHECK` records the decision |
| E-17 | Low | Focus dropped to `<body>` after Remove re-rendered the table; From / To inputs lacked `required` | ui/index.html | **Fixed** |
| E-18 | Low | `_emit_intrazone` swallowed level-resolution exceptions with a bare `continue` (row silently missing) | access_paths.py | **Fixed** - logged with traceback |
| E-19 | Low | Smoke test only exercised the refusal path of the hops panel | tests/ui_smoke_index.py | **Fixed** - table / suggestion counts vs API, exact summary parse, declared-hop text on composed rows, no spurious error |

Considered and left as designed: external → gateway on any remote-access port (incl. 23) is exempt from path 2 - the gateway is the sanctioned entry point and `rule_insecure_protocol` / the Access Review `insecure_protocol` flag still condemn Telnet on the rule; a reversed hop with wide (/24) legs on both sides can compose (the hop is directional by host, the legs are what they are); hop `service` deliberately not intersected with leg 2 (the gateway → jump protocol is not the jump → OT protocol).

## Remediation round 2026-10-05 (everything open except A-01)

Order followed the dependencies: D-11 (labelled interfaces are what chains rest on) → D-05 (chain findings use the enumeration and skip unverified rows) → D-06 / D-07 (topology side) → D-10 → C-20 / C-17 / C-18 / C-19. New check names: `chain_it_ot_bypass`, `chain_mgmt_wrong_zone`. New flag: `direction_unverified`. New migration: b0c1d2e3f4a5 (`firewall_interfaces.segment`). New endpoint: `POST /topology/confirm-segment`. Device type `modbus_device` replaces `plc` for protocol-inferred Modbus servers. Only A-01 remains open.

Engineering round: engineering workstations in the baseline are no longer flagged for SSH / RDP to L1/L2 with no declaration at all; marking an engineering VLAN closes the findings of its hosts, including `unauthorized_industrial_write` findings raised fail-open before the baseline was loaded.

Check: declaring an admin LAN that holds the trusthosts closes nothing new; adding an engineering station as a trusted host closes its management findings (`mgmt_access_from_wrong_zone`, `session_mgmt_from_non_admin`, `mgmt_access_not_restricted`); reclassifying a downlink closes its interface finding; removing declarations brings each back.
